# ATLAS reading packet

Collected 2026-11-22T12:02:06+00:00. This file is context for the editor. It is not the edition.
Each item is a headline already fetched from a feed or a newsroom page. A text-reader item was taken from a page that blocks a normal download.
High confidence is still three independent sources, or five when the national-language and official mix is missing. Medium is two. Low is one, and the summary must say so.

#### Agência Brasil (national_press, pt, feed)

- Sat, 21 Nov 2026 19:39:00 -0300 — Sobe para 4 o número de mortos em Tupanciretã, no Sul
  Morreu neste sábado (21) a quarta vítima da tempestade que devastou a cidade de Tupanciretã , no Rio Grande do Sul, na madrugada desta sexta-feira (20). A vítima é um homem que estava internado no Hospital Universitário de Santa Maria. Os outros três mortos são um homem de 59 anos, outro homem de 84 anos e uma mulher de 85 anos. O município de Tupanciretã, que tem 20 mil habitantes e fica na região central do estado, foi atingido na madrugada de sexta por uma microexplosão. Chuva forte, granizo e ventanias devastaram a cidade e destruíram cerca de 8 mil casas. Além das três mortes iniciais, também 60 pessoas ficaram feridas, segundo a Defesa Civil. 15 mil pessoas foram atingidas no município. Tupanciretã foi a cidade que mais sofreu, mas a tempestade também atingiu outros 33 municípios . A
  https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/meio-ambiente/noticia/2026-11/sobe-para-4-o-numero-de-mortos-em-tupancireta-no-sul

By Matt McKinney It started with about 10 people, a carbon-fiber rocket that skeptics said would never work, and a sheep farm at the end of a windswept New Zealand peninsula. On Nov. 13, 2026, Electron flew for the 100 th time , reaching the mark faster than any other commercially developed rocket in history. Electron, which took roughly four years to develop, now rolls off the factory floor every eight days. What would happen if an asteroid the size of the Great Pyramid of Giza was on a collision course with Earth? Its impact could destroy entire cities, or generate a devastating tsunami if it struck the ocean. Would humanity be prepared to respond to such a threat? That is precisely what the European Space Agency's Hera mission aims to help determine. On September 26 th 2022, NASA’s Double Asteroid Redirection Test (DART) mission carried out humanity’s first-ever asteroid deflection experiment by deliberately crashing its 580 kg spacecraft into Dimorphos, a moonlet measuring 151 meters in diameter, at a speed of 6.1 km/s. Así lo señaló la referente del sector, Alba Pettengill, quien esta semana conversó con La Nación/Nación Media en el espacio de Hacedores LN y sostuvo que este escenario debe ser aprovechado para ampliar la producción y diversificar la oferta nacional . - Sat, 21 Nov 2026 19:39:00 -0300 — Sobe para 4 o número de mortos em Tupanciretã, no Sul
  Morreu neste sábado (21) a quarta vítima da tempestade que devastou a cidade de Tupanciretã , no Rio Grande do Sul, na madrugada desta sexta-feira (20). A vítima é um homem que estava internado no Hospital Universitário de Santa Maria. Os outros três mortos são um homem de 59 anos, outro homem de 84 anos e uma mulher de 85 anos. O município de Tupanciretã, que tem 20 mil habitantes e fica na região central do estado, foi atingido na madrugada de sexta por uma microexplosão. Chuva forte, granizo e ventanias devastaram a cidade e destruíram cerca de 8 mil casas. Além das três mortes iniciais, também 60 pessoas ficaram feridas, segundo a Defesa Civil. 15 mil pessoas foram atingidas no município. Tupanciretã foi a cidade que mais sofreu, mas a tempestade também atingiu outros 33 municípios . A
  https://agenciabrasil.ebc.com.br/meio-ambiente/noticia/2026-11/sobe-para-4-o-numero-de-mortos-em-tupancireta-no-sul

  https://www.federalregister.gov/documents/2026/11/23/2026-23838/establishment-of-class-e-airspace-columbus-tx
- Mon, 23 Nov 2026 05:00:00 GMT — Amendment of Class E Airspace; Del Rio, TX
  This action amends the Class E airspace at Del Rio, TX. This action is the result of a biennial review of the airspace associated with Laughlin Air Force Base (AFB), Del Rio, TX. This proposal also updates the name and geographic coordinates of Laughlin AFB to coincide with the FAA&#39;s aeronautical database. This action brings the airspace into compliance with FAA orders and supports instrument flight rule (IFR) procedures and operations.
  https://www.federalregister.gov/documents/2026/11/23/2026-23836/amendment-of-class-e-airspace-del-rio-tx
- Mon, 23 Nov 2026 05:00:00 GMT — Amendment of Class D and E Airspace and Removal of Class E Airspace Over Lebanon, NH
  This action proposes to amend Class D and Class E airspace over Lebanon, NH. This action would increase the lateral dimensions of the Lebanon, NH, Class D airspace to appropriately contain Instrument Flight Rules (IFR) operations at the Lebanon Municipal Airport. This action would also update verbiage in the Class D airspace legal description to comply with current FAA guidance. This action would also update the geographic coordinates for the airport in both the Class D and Class E5 airspace legal descriptions. This action would also remove the exclusions for adjacent Class E5 airspace from the Lebanon, NH, Class E5 airspace legal description. This action would also remove the Lebanon, NH, Class E4 airspace, which is no longer necessary for IFR operations at the airport.
  https://www.federalregister.gov/documents/2026/11/23/2026-23824/amendment-of-class-d-and-e-airspace-and-removal-of-class-e-airspace-over-lebanon-nh

Operators are encouraged to review the&nbsp; TraCSS User Agreement &amp; Data Policy &nbsp;prior to signing up. Note that at this&nbsp;time, TraCSS accounts are only available to organizations that own and/or operate spacecraft in orbit, and National Government users .   https://space.commerce.gov/traffic-coordination-system-for-space-tracss/tracss-registration-for-spacecraft-operators/
- undated — Dataset for Conjunction Assessment Verification
  Dataset for Conjunction Assessment Verification &#8211; Office of Space Commerce &darr; Skip to Main Content Background: This test set was developed for the Traffic Coordination System for Space (TraCSS) and is designed to help space situational awareness (SSA) service providers test their conjunction assessment (CA) algorithms. The dataset comes with an accompanying &#8220;answer key&#8221; that lists the conjunctions in the dataset &#8211; providing a voluntary tool for self-assessment. DISCLAIMER : While this ephemeris dataset was crafted to include stressing edge cases in addition to an operational catalog, it is not completely comprehensive (as there are nearly infinite combinations of orbits and conjunction geometries possible).   https://space.commerce.gov/dataset-for-conjunction-assessment-verification/

  TraCSS-EU SST Joint Studies &amp; Service Comparison &#8211; Office of Space Commerce &darr; Skip to Main Content Background Space debris and collision avoidance are challenges for all spacecraft, regardless of their nation of registry. As laid out in the Office of Space Commerce’s&nbsp; Vision for Global SSA Coordination , international collaboration is key to enhancing data consistency, coverage, and transparency. Both the Traffic Coordination System for Space (TraCSS) and the European Union Space Surveillance and Tracking (EU SST), in partnership with the commercial space situational awareness (SSA) industry, will offer free space SSA services to spacecraft operators worldwide to prevent in-orbit collisions. #### Payload (space_press, en, newsroom)

- undated — Atomic-6 Wants to Shield Astronauts Against Untrackable Debris
  Atomic-6 Wants to Shield Astronauts Against Untrackable Debris Skip to content What’s the scariest thing about space exploration? Probably the snakes . The second most frightening thing? Untrackable debris. Atomic-6 built Space Armor to shield satellites from debris too small to track. The startup released test results today exploring whether the same tiles could protect astronauts on spacewalks. “Building Space Armor, we want to protect assets in space,” Atomic-6 CEO Trevor Smith told Payload. “I can’t think of a higher value asset than a human life.” Bullseye: At Texas A&amp;M University’s Hypervelocity Impact Laboratory, Atomic-6 fired a 3-mm aluminum sphere speeding ~7.2 km/s (~16,100 mph) at a ballistic torso—once behind a Space Armor Lite tile and once behind a 4-mm-thick solid aluminum plate. Atomic-6 said the plate stands in for current spacesuit protection. “No humans were harme
  https://payloadspace.com/atomic-6-wants-to-shield-astronauts-against-untrackable-debris/
- undated — RBC Signals Raises $10M to Commercialize New Comms Tech
  RBC Signals Raises $10M to Commercialize New Comms Tech Skip to content RBC Signals, the WA-based ground station network provider, raised a $10M round led by Washington Harbour Partners, the company announced yesterday.&nbsp; The fresh funds are intended to expand the company’s government services business, and scale its product lineup to commercialize capabilities such as intersatellite links, optical ground comms, SDA, and spectrum monitoring. Hearing footsteps: The new capital comes amid a period of rapid expansion for RBC, which has spent the last few years developing new partnerships and technologies to increase both the coverage and capabilities of its ground station network. In 2025, RBC Signals acquired 10 S- and X-band tracking antennas from Microsoft to expand its global ground station capacity. The same year, Space Force selected the company for its Joint Antenna Marketplace.
  https://payloadspace.com/rbc-signals-raises-10m-to-commercialize-new-comms-tech/
- undated — European Hardware Takes Center Stage at Space Tech Expo
  European Hardware Takes Center Stage at Space Tech Expo Skip to content Europe is a region chock full of builders. At least, that’s the vibe we got this week watching the news wire from Space Tech Expo in Bremen, Germany. Like all space conferences, the Expo coincided with a firehose of announcements, but one theme stood out: Europe is building a metric ship-load of hardware. Production lineup: For well over a year, Payload Europe has covered the growth in manufacturing capacity coming from the region’s satellite manufacturers, and components suppliers. In all, Europe is on the verge of being able to produce high-hundreds of satellites annually, and demand from programs like IRIS 2 will require at least as much.&nbsp; Space Tech Expo proved that Europe’s manufacturing boom is still on an upswing—and it extends far beyond the production of satellite buses and subcomponents.   https://payloadspace.com/european-hardware-takes-center-stage-at-space-tech-expo/

- undated — Voyager 1 Reaches New Milestone: One Light-Day from Earth
  Voyager 1 Reaches New Milestone: One Light-Day from Earth &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com NASA&#8217;s Voyager 1 spacecraft achieved another milestone this morning. Launched in 1977, it became the first human object to pass into interstellar space in August 2012 and is so far from Earth now that it takes one entire day for a radio signal to get there &#8212; one light-day. It also takes one light-day for Voyager 1 to send back the data it is still acquiring after all these decades. Voyager 1 passed the one light-day threshold at 5:16:07 am ET this morning at a distance of 16.094 billion miles (25.902 billion kilometers). That means it takes two days to communicate with Voyager &#8212; one day for the signal to get there and another day for the answer to come back. The spacecraft sent back stunning images of Jupiter in 1979 and Saturn in 1980. Since then it&#8217;s been on a quest to find w
  https://spacepolicyonline.com/news/voyager-1-reaches-new-milestone-one-light-day-from-earth/

- undated — Insufficient Fuel Flow May Have Doomed Vulcan
  Insufficient Fuel Flow May Have Doomed Vulcan &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com The United Launch Alliance said today that insufficient fuel flow from the launch vehicle to the BE-4 engines may have led to the failure of Vulcan Leo 1 on Sunday. The fifth flight of ULA&#8217;s new Vulcan rocket failed about 10 minutes after liftoff on November 15, the company&#8217;s first total failure in 20 years of operation. By yesterday they had traced the problem to &#8220;low booster performance,&#8221; referring to the rocket&#8217;s first stage. The first stage is powered by two BE-4 liquid methane/liquid oxygen (methalox) engines built by Blue Origin, which uses them for its own New Glenn rocket. In an update today, ULA says preliminary indications are that it was not the engines themselves, but &#8220;inadequate fuel supply from the vehicle&#8221; to the engines.   Voyager 1 reaches one light-day from Earth Close Search for: Search Close Skip to content Don Davis's painting Voyager 1 Light Day shows the spacecraft at one light-day from Earth, with the Sun a bright point among the stars. Credit: Don Davis NASA&#8217;s Voyager 1 crossed one light-day from Earth early Wednesday, becoming the first human-made object to travel so far that a radio signal, moving at the speed of light, needs a full 24 hours to reach it. This moment, this milestone, though not relevant in our busy daily lives, is something to take a moment to consider. It is, after all, a significant human achievement, one that even 100 years ago would have seemed like science fiction. And that significance is heightened when you consider that the spacecraft has been working for 49 years. - undated — Defensie bouwt aan Space Command Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting ... 30-06-2026 17:00 Lees verder
  Defensie bouwt aan Space Command | Rijksoverheid.nl Nieuwsbericht 30-06-2026 | 17:00 Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting van het Space Command bouwt Defensie verder aan het vijfde operationele domein (ruimte) naast land, zee, lucht en cyber. Dit is aangekondigd in de gisteren gepresenteerde Defensienota. Het doel is om in 2028 volledig operationeel te zijn met het Space Command . De basis voor militaire ruimteactiviteiten werd ruim 10 jaar geleden gelegd met kleinschalige experimenten. Inmiddels is het ruimtedomein uitgegroeid tot een operationeel onderdeel van Defensie, onder meer dankzij de inzet van eigen observatiesatellieten. Ruimte werd vorig jaar officieel ingericht als vijfde operationele domein. Daarom volgde de transitie van het Commando Luchtstrijdkrachten (CLSK) naar het Commando Lucht

  A little more than four years ago, humans moved an asteroid, as NASA’s DART spacecraft impacted a body called Dimorphos. - undated — Chi mangia patate (lunari) non muore mai
  Chi mangia patate (lunari) non muore mai &#8211; MEDIA INAF Una delle principali sfide dell’esplorazione spaziale dei prossimi decenni è stabilire una presenza umana stabile e duratura sul nostro satellite naturale, un obiettivo a cui punta il programma Artemis della Nasa, che mira anche a gettare le basi per la futura esplorazione di Marte. Affinché ciò sia possibile, sarà tuttavia necessario garantire agli astronauti un approvvigionamento alimentare sostenibile. La permanenza prolungata sulla Luna dipenderà infatti anche dalla possibilità di produrre cibo direttamente sul posto, riducendo la necessità di costose missioni di rifornimento dalla Terra. Patate ottenute negli esperimenti condotti da David Handy e colleghi in un terreno simulante la regolite lunare. #### StartMag (press, it, text-reader)

- undated — Cosa prevede il nuovo Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale
  Nel corso della seduta del Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), il governo Meloni ha approvato Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale. Tutti i dettagli Palazzo Chigi accelera su autonomia strategica e space economy. Ieri il governo Meloni ha approvato il nuovo Documento Strategico di Politica Spaziale Nazionale (Dspsn), predisposto dall’Agenzia Spaziale Italiana (Asi) in coordinamento con il Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), in attuazione degli Indirizzi del Governo in materia spaziale e aerospaziale. In Italia, il coordinamento politico dello spazio è gestito infatti dalla Presidenza del Consiglio dei ministri, con il Presidente del Consiglio che presiede il Comint. Il piano appena approvato definirà le priorità italiane nello spazio per il prossimo decennio. In particolare, il documento individua quattro assi strategici – ricerca e benefici per la società, competitività industriale, governance e cooperazione internazionale – e punta a rafforzare l’autonomia strategica del Paese, la sicurezza delle infrastrutture spaziali e la leadership industriale italian
  https://www.startmag.it/spazio-e-difesa/cosa-prevede-il-nuovo-documento-strategico-di-politica-spaziale-nazionale/

- undated — Was genau ist ein CubeSat? : Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders?
  Was genau ist ein CubeSat? - Austria in Space Was genau ist ein CubeSat? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders? Früher waren Satelliten immer riesengroße, tonnenschwere Raumfahrzeuge, die ein hohes Budget, viel Zeit und viel Expertise verlangt haben. Nur wenige Nationen konntent sich solche Raumfahrt-Technologie leisten, was zur Folge hatte, dass es nur eine handvoll Weltraumnationen gab. Heute ist das anders, und das liegt an einem Würfel mit 10 cm Seitenlänge! Richtig, die Rede ist von einem CubeSat. Ein CubeSat, das ist ein kleiner Satellit, der aus einem oder mehreren würfelförmigen Modulen mit eben jeweils nur 10 cm Seitenlänge. Dabei sind diese Raumfahrzeuge nicht nur klein, sondern auch sehr leicht: Eine Einheit wiegt maximal 1,33 Kilo.
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/geschichte/was-sind-cubesats.php
- undated — Was versteht man unter Spacetech? : Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau?
  Was versteht man unter Spacetech? - Austria in Space Was versteht man unter Spacetech? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau? Jeder redet von Space Technology, aber was ist das überhaupt?&nbsp; Der Weltraumsektor ist im Wesentlichen zweigeteilt: In Upstream und in Downstream. Der Upstream Bereich umfasst die Beförderung von Objekten in den Weltraum, Bodenstationen und die Weltraumforschung, während als Downstream Daten und Services bezeichnet werden, die sich aus dem Upstream, vor allem aus Satelliten, ergeben.&nbsp;Eine Vielzahl an Anwendungen für die Erde fallen daher in den Downstream Bereich. Gerade darin liegt auch die Stärke des österreichischen Weltraumsektors. Doch auch europaweit liegt dort das größte Potential für die Wirtsch
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/artikel/was-ist-spacetech.php

#### BMIMI (Federal Ministry for Innovation, Mobility and Infrastructure) (government, de, newsroom)

- undated — Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen
  Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Das Weltraumbüro (UNOOSA) und der Weltraumausschuss (UNCOPUOS) wurden 1958 von der Generalversammlung der Vereinten Nationen gegründet. Ziel des Ausschusses ist es, die friedliche internationale Zusammenarbeit im Weltraum zu fördern, die Erforschung des Weltraums zu unterstützen, sowie das Weltraumrecht weiterzuentwickeln. Österreich ist seit 1959 Mitglied des Weltraumausschusses. Seit 1993 befinden sich das Büro und der Ausschuss in Wien. Jährlich treffen sich Delegierte aus mittlerweile 100 Mitgliedsstaaten in Wien, um aktuelle und zukünftige Themen und Entwicklungen der Weltraumforschung und -nutzung zu diskutieren.&#xa0; Das Bundesministerium unterstützt die Arbeiten des Weltraumbüros insbesondere mit dem jährlich stattfindenden United Nations/Austria Symposium in Graz, sowie dem World Space Forum.
  https://www.bmimi.gv.at/themen/innovation/fti-themen/weltraum-und-luftfahrttechnologien/weltraum/weltraumbuero.html

  24 déc 2025 -16:21 > Appartient à Conseil des ministres du 23 décembre 2025 ## Programmation spatiale belge pour la période 2026-2030 Sur proposition de la ministre chargée de la Politique scientifique Vanessa Matz, le Conseil des ministres a pris acte des récents développements dans le secteur spatial international et des opportunités qui s'offrent aux entreprises spatiales belges dans ce même contexte. Il prend par ailleurs plusieurs décisions destinées à renforcer le secteur spatial belge. Les 26 et 27 novembre s’est tenue à Brême la réunion du conseil de l’Agence spatiale européenne (ESA) au niveau ministériel, qui couvrait tous les domaines d’activités pour la période 2026-2030. Au cours de cette réunion, les États membres étaient invités à adopter quatre résolutions et à s’inscrire aux différents programmes pour les cinq prochaines années. Dans ce contexte, le Conseil des ministres : approuve le cofinancement par la Défense de la participation belge aux programmes de l’ESA a dégagé 400 millions d’euros lors de l’élaboration du budget pluriannuel afin de permettre aux inscriptions belges d'atteindre un niveau satisfaisant et de d’octroyer à l'écosystème spatial belge suffisamm

- undated — tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie in 2025
  Nederland verhoogt inzet tijdens ESA-ministersconferentie in Bremen - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Minister van Economische Zaken, Vincent Karremans, op ESA-ministerconferentie Nederland verhoogt de bijdrage aan de Europese ruimtevaartorganisatie ESA met 109 miljoen euro. Dit kondigde minister Karremans van Economische Zaken vandaag aan tijdens de ESA ministersconferentie in Bremen. De totale Nederlandse inzet tijdens de conferentie komt daarmee op 453 miljoen euro. NSO-directeur Harm van de Wetering reageert: “Hiermee zet Nederland een belangrijke stap in lijn met de politiek gesteunde Langetermijn-ruimtevaartagenda.” Eens in de drie jaar komen de voor ruimtevaart verantwoordelijke ministers van ESA-lidstaten bij elkaar tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie. Daar besluiten ze over de toekomst van de Europese ruimtevaart. Dit jaar vond de conferentie plaats in Bremen. Op
  https://www.nlsa.nl/nederland-verhoogt-inzet-tijdens-esa-ministersconferentie-in-bremen/
- undated — 18 november 2026 20 jaar ESERO Onderwijs: ruimtevaart als vonk voor toekomstig talent Lees meer
  20 jaar ESERO Onderwijs: ruimtevaart als vonk voor toekomstig talent - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Zelf een minisatelliet bouwen, trainen als een astronaut of de satelliet van de toekomst ontwerpen. Lessen en activiteiten van ESERO Onderwijs brengen al twintig jaar ruimtevaart in de klas. Gisteren vierde het programma zijn jubileum bij ESTEC in Noordwijk. ESERO onderwijs begon met een pilot in 2006. Na een succesvol scholenexperiment tijdens de eerste ruimtemissie van André Kuipers – bijna 80.000 leerlingen deden mee aan seeds in space – sloegen ESA en de Nederlandse overheid de handen ineen om werk te maken van ruimtevaarteducatie. Ruimtevaart kan scholieren inspireren en op het pad zetten van een carrière in wetenschap of techniek, was ook de overtuiging van het Ministerie van Onderwijs, Cultuur en Wetenschap. &nbsp; Twintig jaar later is ESERO onderwijs uitgegroeid

  Vertrag unterzeichnet: VBS erhält Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich Vertrag mit Frankreich Amherd hat bald den Überblick Der Schweizer «Blindflug» ist bald zu Ende. Der Bund kann künftig Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich beziehen. Ein entsprechender Rahmenvertrag wurde unterzeichnet. Publiziert: 29.06.2023 um 11:12 Uhr | Aktualisiert: 29.06.2023 um 11:56 Uhr Teilen Kommentieren 1/5 Die Schweiz kann künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO beziehen (Symbolbild). Foto: AP Verteidigungsministerin Viola Amherd (61) macht einen weiteren Schritt auf das westliche Verteidigungsbündnis Nato zu. Soeben haben Frankreich und die Schweiz einen Rahmenvertrag unterzeichnet, der es dem Bund ermöglicht, künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO «Composante Spatiale Optique» zu beziehen. Selber hat die Schweiz keine Satelliten im All. Desha

#### Spektrum der Wissenschaft (spektrum.de) (science, de, newsroom)

- undated — Montage im All | Roboter soll riesige Strukturen im Orbit bauen
  Montage im All: Roboter soll riesige Strukturen im Orbit bauen - Spektrum der Wissenschaft Direkt zum Inhalt Startseite IT/Tech Aktuelle Seite: Montage im All: Roboter soll riesige Strukturen im Orbit bauen News 19.11.2026 Lesedauer ca. 1 Minute Drucken Teilen Spektrum.de als bevorzugte Quelle einrichten – bei Google, öffnet in neuem Tab © DARPA | Colie Wertz (Ausschnitt) In Zukunft könnten sich Tragwerke von Hunderten Metern Durchmesser in der Erdumlaufbahn errichten lassen. Wer größere Strukturen von einer Rakete ins All transportieren lassen will, hat zwei Möglichkeiten: Entweder man legt sie vor dem Start zusammen und sorgt dafür, dass sie sich im Weltall wieder entfalten – Beispiel Sonnensegel –, oder man setzt Teile, die über mehrere Starts geliefert werden, nach und nach aneinander. So geschieht es etwa beim Bau von Raumstationen . Ein Team vom Forschungsinstitut Caltech in Kalifo
  https://www.spektrum.de/news/montage-im-all-roboter-soll-riesige-strukturen-im-orbit-bauen/2354932

  --> HELLAS–SPACE 2.0: Προκηρύχθηκε ο πρώτος διαγωνισμός ύψους 69 εκατ. ευρώ – Η Ελλάδα αποκτά νέους οπτικούς δορυφόρους πολύ υψηλής ανάλυσης - ertnews.gr Μετάβαση σε περιεχόμενο Στην προκήρυξη του πρώτου διαγωνισμού του Εθνικού Διαστημικού Προγράμματος « HELLAS–SPACE 2.0 », προϋπολογισμού 69 εκατ. ευρώ, προχωρά το Υπουργείο Ψηφιακής Διακυβέρνησης και Τεχνητής Νοημοσύνης . Το έργο αφορά στην ανάπτυξη εθνικού αστερισμού τριών οπτικών δορυφόρων Παρατήρησης της Γης πολύ υψηλής χωρικής ανάλυσης, με στόχο την ενίσχυση της αυτονομίας της χώρας στην πρόσβαση σε δορυφορικά δεδομένα και την κάλυψη επιχειρησιακών αναγκών των Δημόσιων Υπηρεσιών. Ο διαγωνισμός διενεργείται από τον Ευρωπαϊκό Οργανισμό Διαστήματος (ESA), σε συνεργασία με το Υπουργείο Ψηφιακής Διακυβέρνησης και Τεχνητής Νοημοσύνης και το Ελληνικό Κέντρο Διαστήματος (ΕΛΚΕΔ). Publicité » --> La création du Bureau des affaires spatiales monégasques vient renforcer l&rsquo;attrait de la Principauté pour ce secteur. Monaco gagne du terrain dans un domaine en pleine expansion : l’activité aérospatiale privée. Le 12 octobre dernier, le Gouvernement Princier a annoncé la création du Bureau des affaires spatiales, conformément à la volonté du Prince Albert II de diversifier l’économie et de renforcer l’attractivité de la Principauté. L’enjeu est triple : accompagner les entreprises du domaine spatial déjà implantées sur le territoire monégasque, créer une base attractive pour celles qui veulent s’installer en Principauté et représenter Monaco à l’inte

Bojový pilot Aleš Svoboda uspěl v náročném výběrovém řízení Evropské kosmické agentury, kam se hlásilo víc než 22 tisíc lidí z Evropy. Svoboda je astronautem v záloze a teď mu svitla reálná naděje, že se do vesmíru skutečně podívá v rámci některé z misí Axiom. Jeho cestu podporuje vláda i premiér Petr Fiala. - undated — Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space
  Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space Menu It has become a truism that Europe’s defence industry and military systems are excessively fragmented, and therefore often more costly and less efficient than they would be if pooled at the European, or at least regional, level. Yet despite repeated warnings and countless recommendations, the continent now appears set to repeat the same mistake in space security, leaving itself particularly exposed at precisely the moment when space is becoming more strategically important than ever. European space capabilities are already deeply fragmented, especially in the defence sector. In contrast to continent-wide civilian programmes such as Galileo or Copernicus, military space assets remain scattered across the armed forces and governments of individual states. As a result, just four countries — France, Germany, Italy, and Spain — operate
  https://defence24.com/geopolitics/europe-may-be-repeating-its-biggest-mistake-in-space
- undated — The Baltic as a ‘battlespace’: When diplomacy and Special Operations Forces work together [OPINION]
  The Baltic as a ‘battlespace’: When diplomacy and Special Operations Forces work together [OPINION] Menu Following Russia’s full-scale invasion of Ukraine in 2022, Finland and Sweden decided to join the North Atlantic Alliance. It was at that point that the Baltic Sea began to be described, in simplified media shorthand, as a so-called »NATO inland sea«. For analysts and planners, however, it remains a maritime area riddled with gaps — in legislation, procedures and the architecture for sharing information. The recent ORKAN 26 exercise sought to identify those gaps before an adversary could exploit them. Poland's Presidency of the Council of the Baltic Sea States ORKAN 26 was organised by the Polish Special Operations Component Command and the Ministry of Foreign Affairs as a major event within the programme of Poland’s presidency of the Council of the Baltic Sea States. - undated — NASA przyspiesza budowę bazy na Księżycu. Miliardowe kontrakty na infrastrukturę
  NASA przyspiesza budowę bazy na Księżycu. Miliardowe kontrakty na infrastrukturę Menu Ustanowienie stałej obecności człowieka na Księżycu jest znacznie trudniejszym zadaniem niż tymczasowy pobyt. Wymaga to nie tylko sprowadzenia na jego powierzchnię samych astronautów, ale także zagwarantowania im niezakłóconego dostępu do energii, zapasów i komunikacji. Czyni to z budowy bazy księżycowej przede wszystkim wyzwanie infrastrukturalne, w którym dostarczenie kluczowych ładunków i elementów bazy wysuwa się na pierwszy plan. Świadoma tego faktu amerykańska agencja kosmiczna przyspiesza wysiłki na rzecz realizacji ogłoszonego w marcu 2026 r. programu budowy bazy księżycowej do 2032 r., którego koszt szacuje się na 20 mld USD. Na początku listopada NASA ogłosiła kolejne kontrakty z amerykańskimi przedsiębiorstwami na dostarczenie kluczowych elementów infrastruktury transportowej, komunikacyjnej

  Sodelovanje z Evropsko vesoljsko agencijo | GOV.SI Domov Področja Podjetništvo in gospodarstvo Inovativnost in tehnološki razvoj Sodelovanje z Evropsko vesoljsko agencijo Slovenija je od 1. januarja 2025 polnopravna članica Evropske vesoljske agencije (angleško The European Space Agency – Esa), kar slovenskim podjetjem in razvojnoraziskovalnim institucijam omogoča sodelovanje pri projektih Ese na področju vesoljskih tehnologij. Sodelujejo lahko podjetja in institucije, ki se ukvarjajo z inovativnimi materiali, tehnologijami, procesi ali informacijskimi rešitvami. Priložnost za sodelovanje z Eso imajo tudi mala in srednja podjetja, kar potrjujejo uspešni projekti podjetij iz Slovenije, ki so to priložnost že izkoristila. Velik korak za civilizacijo, je podvig komentiral generalni direktor Evropske vesoljske postaje. Gre za prvi pristanek na kometu v zgodovini človeštva in zgodovinski dosežek Evropske vesoljske agencije (ESA). Do pristanka je prišlo nekaj minut po peti popoldne po našem času. Po ločitvi Philae in Rosette so znanstveniki v Nemčiji prejeli signal z Rosette, ki jim je začela pošiljati podatke, in tudi s Philae. Philae so tako lahko sledili vso pot do površja kometa. Zgodovinski poskus pristanka na kometu s premerom več kot štiri kilometre se je odvijal več kot 500 milijonov kilometrov stran od Zemlje.   Satellitter, infrastruktur og statlige romaktører Til hovedinnhold Satellitter, infrastruktur og statlige romaktører Norge har bygget opp en rominfrastruktur både til kommersiell og offentlig bruk. Norge har vært en romnasjon siden Andøya rakettskytefelt skjøt opp den første forskningsraketten i 1962. Siden har vi bygget opp rominfrastruktur tilpasset landets behov til en hver tid. Vårt medlemskap i ESA og samarbeid med EU har fått stor betydning for dette. Etter småsatellittenes inntog har Norge også bygget opp en flåte av egne nasjonale satellitter som skal dekke behov som vi ikke får dekket gjennom internasjonale samabeid. Nasjonale satellitter AISSat-1 (2010-2022) AISSat-1 var en pionér i Norge og i verden. Eier Direktoratet for romvirksomhet Hovedoppgave Skipsovervåking Dimensjoner 20x20x20 cm. 6 kg.   Norge og internasjonalt samarbeid Til hovedinnhold Norge og internasjonalt samarbeid Bærebjelken i norsk satsing på romvirksomhet er deltakelse i internasjonalt samarbeid. I første rekke gjennom den europeiske romorganisasjonen ESA og EUs romprogram. I tillegg har Norge samarbeidsavtaler med enkeltland. Norge og ESA Den europeiske romorganisasjonen (ESA) er en internasjonal forsknings- og utviklingsorganisasjon grunnlagt i 1975. ESAs formål er å fremme samarbeid mellom europeiske stater innen romforskning og romteknologi for vitenskapelige formål og for utvikling av operasjonsdyktige romanvendelsessystemer. Samarbeid med ESA ESA er den Europeiske romorganisasjonen, og gjennom delegatvirksomheten administrerer Romsenteret Norges deltagelse i ESA. Senators Ted Cruz, R-Texas, and Peter Welch, D-Vermont, introduced the Satellite and Telecommunications Streamlining Act on January 14 which would set a one-year deadline for the FCC to make a decision on a license application.   ### 1. Citation This Order may be cited as the Kenya Space Agency Order. ### 2. Interpretation In this Order—"Act" means the State Corporations Act (Cap. 466);"Agency" means the Kenya Space Agency established under paragraph 3;"Board" means the Board of the Agency appointed under paragraph 6;"Director-General" means the person appointed under paragraph 11;"Cabinet Secretary" means the Cabinet Secretary for the time being responsible for Defence;"Committee" means the State Corporations Advisory Committee;"member" means a person appointed to the Board under paragraph 6(1)(i). ## Part II – ESTABLISHMENT, POWERS AND FUNCTIONS OF KENYA SPACE AGENCY ### 3.   https://new.kenyalaw.org/akn/ke/act/ln/2017/22

- undated — Angola e Emirados Árabes Unidos reforçam cooperação no sector espacial
  Angola e Emirados Árabes Unidos reforçam cooperação no sector espacial Angola e Emirados Árabes Unidos reforçam cooperação no sector espacial Sep 25, 2026 Destaques GGPEN e Agência Espacial dos Emirados Árabes Unidos assinam Carta de Intenção para promover colaboração em satélites, observação da Terra, capacitação e inovação. O Gabinete de Gestão do Programa Espacial Angolano (GGPEN) e a Agência Espacial dos Emirados Árabes Unidos (UAE Space Agency) assinaram, esta quinta-feira, 25 de Setembro, em Abidjan, Côte d’Ivoire, uma Carta de Intenção que estabelece as bases para o reforço da cooperação entre Angola e os Emirados Árabes Unidos no domínio espacial. O documento foi assinado pelo Director-Geral do GGPEN, Dr. Zolana Rui João, e pelo Director-Geral da Agência Espacial dos Emirados Árabes Unidos, Eng. Salem Butti Salem Al Qubaisi, à margem da Africa Space Expo (ASPEX) 2026, onde Angola
  https://ggpen.gov.ao/en/news/angola-e-emirados-arabes-unidos-reforcam-cooperacao-no-sector-espacial
- undated — MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate
  MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate Sep 06, 2026 Destaques O Space Enabled Research Group, integrado no MIT Media Lab do Massachusetts Institute of Technology (MIT), estendeu a afiliação do angolano Dr. Zolana João como Research Affiliate (Investigador Afiliado), vínculo que mantém com o grupo desde 2023. A decisão dá continuidade a uma colaboração científica desenvolvida ao longo dos últimos anos com uma das instituições académicas de maior referência mundial nas áreas de ciência, tecnologia e engenharia. A extensão da afiliação reforça a presença de Angola em redes internacionais de investigação e inovação e reconhece a contribuição de investigadores angolanos para a produção de conhecimento sobre a utilização responsável, inclusiva e sustentável das tecno
  https://ggpen.gov.ao/en/news/mit-space-enabled-estende-afiliacao-do-angolano-zolana-joao-como-research-affiliate
- undated — Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space
  Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Jul 23, 2026 Destaques Foi lançado, no dia 13 de Julho de 2026, o projecto Conecta Angola TVWS, uma iniciativa do MINTTICS, através do GGPEN e parceiros, que combina as capacidades do satélite ANGOSAT-2 com a tecnologia TV White Space (TVWS) para levar internet de banda larga a comunidades com acesso limitado à conectividade. A solução utiliza frequências não ocupadas do espectro televisivo para fornecer internet de forma eficiente, segura e com alcance de até 10 quilómetros, permitindo ampliar a cobertura digital e criar novas oportunidades de acesso a plataformas educativas, conteúdos digitais e serviços tecnológicos.   NEREUS Training &#8211; A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System &#8211; Mauritius Research and Innovation Council Skip to content Menu This training is the third and last of a series of courses within the Capacity Building programme for the NEREUS project funded by the United Nations Development Programme (UNDP) Ocean Innovation Challenge ( https://oceaninnovationchallenge.org ). The NEREUS project, led by the Surrey Space Centre (SSC) in collaboration with the Mauritius Research and Innovation Council (MRIC), aims at developing a tool, based on a Machine Learning algorithm to detect anomalies in maritime satellite data. Analysing these anomalies is very important in ensuring maritime security and environmental protection.   https://spacemauritius.com/nereus-training-a-space-based-maritime-surveillance-system/

  Contre les dérives en ligne, Lomé mise sur la sensibilisation - République Togolaise Tendances: Diplomatie Lire la suite Les plateformes restent hors d’atteinte © repoublicoftogo.com La Direction de la formation civique (DFC) a lancé une campagne de sensibilisation destinée aux internautes togolais. Son objectif est de lutter contre les dérives sur les réseaux sociaux : insultes et diffamation. « Les réseaux sociaux ne doivent pas devenir des espaces de dénigrement et d’atteinte à la réputation d’autrui », rappellent les autorités. Le constat est juste. Les échanges en ligne virent souvent à l’affrontement, et les attaques personnelles se multiplient. Le phénomène n’est pas propre au Togo : il touche le monde entier. Reste à savoir ce que peut changer une campagne de sensibilisation. Les intentions sont bonnes, mais elles relèvent largement du vœu pieux. Le premier obstacle est technique

ZIMSAT-2 Zimbabwe&#8217;s second satellite mission, building on the success of ZIMSAT-1 and expanding national capabilities in space technology and Earth observation. CubeSat Development Advanced CubeSat development using 3D printing technology and modern engineering practices to build indigenous small-satellite capability. - undated — 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について New!
  国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について : 宇宙政策 - 内閣府 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について 令和８年９月１日 内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局 １．概要 令和８年８月31日（月）、龍崎孝嗣内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局長は、「内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局と米国連邦航空局（FAA）※との間の国際商業宇宙輸送に係る協力覚書」に署名を行いました。本協力覚書には、日本側から龍崎局長が署名を行い、米国側からベッドフォード連邦航空局長官が署名を行いました。 覚書締結に合わせて、龍崎局長から、本協力覚書を通じて、日米両国の法令や基準の調和を図り、日米共に更に安全で信頼性の高い商業宇宙輸送の拡大を目指す旨発言しました。ベッドフォード長官からも、引き続き連携を深めていく意向が示され、今後の協力の継続及び定期的な会合の実施を両者で確認しました。 ※米国連邦航空局（Federal Aviation Administration：FAA）は、米国における商業宇宙輸送活動の奨励、円滑化及び促進を図るため、商業宇宙輸送を規制する権限を有している。 ２．協力覚書の目的 本協力覚書は、商業宇宙輸送に適用されるベストプラクティスの共有及び産業標準の推進を行い、相互の関心事項及びプロジェクトに関して協力することを目的としています。 ３．対象とする協力活動概要 本協力覚書が推進する協力は、主に以下の項目です。 法令、規制、政策、プログラム及びプロジェクトに関する情報交換等 宇宙機の打上げ及び再突入、サブオービタル飛行、有人宇宙飛行並びに宇宙港に関する安全基準、リスク管理基準及び適合性評価における共通事項の検討 ベストプラクティスの共有 シンポジウム、ワークショップ、会議又はワーキンググループの共同開催 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について このページの先頭へ
  https://www8.cao.go.jp/space/kokusai/20260901/260901.html

- undated — GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit.
  GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION Home / GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit. October 28, 2026 GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 is the 20 th flight of India’s Geosynchronous Satellite Launch Vehicle (GSLV). The GSLV-F18 vehicle placed NVS-03 satellite into a Geosynchronous Transfer Orbit.The Launch was successfully executed from the Second Launch Pad (SLP) at Satish Dhawan Space Centre, SHAR. NVS-03 is the Third Satellite in Next generation navigation satellite system envisaged for providing continuity of navigation services for IRNSS series. The satellite carries Indigenous Atomic clock, along with the procured Atomic clocks. It also includes L1 signal in addition to existing L5/S signals with Coverage over India land mass and a region extending 1500 km around Indian geopolitical boundary. GSLV-F18 on the launch pad More De
  https://www.isro.gov.in/Mission_GSLVF18.html

November 13, 2026 International Committee on Global Navigation Satellite Systems (ICG), established in 2005 under the umbrella of the United Nations is an important forum to promote voluntary cooperation on matters of mutual interest related to civil satellite-based positioning, navigation, timing, and value-added services. ISRO/DoS had the privilege to host the 20 th annual meeting of ICG (ICG-20) from October 25 to 30, 2026 along with the United Nations Office for Outer Space Affairs (UNOOSA) at Goa, India. Subsequent to the anomalies observed in the third stage of PSLV during the PSLV-C61/EOS-09 & PSLV-C62 / EOS-N1 missions, the configuration of the PS3 stage was revisited comprehensively w.r.t the design margins, raw material acceptance criteria, assembly procedures, and the quality checks that were followed so far.   Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year Business Nasa to boost space mission in Thailand next year WEDNESDAY, DECEMBER 07, 2022 สลับเป็นเสียงผู้ชาย Play Loading... Thailand is tapping Nasa expertise to set up its northern “space lab” in Chiang Rai province. A Nasa delegation has been invited to visit the Space Technology Laboratory at Wiangpapao Technical College on January 25 to exchange knowledge on the development of space affairs. The knowledge exchange was agreed at a videoconference meeting between Nasa officials and the House committee on communications, telecoms and digital economy and society,its vice chair Settapong Malisuwan said on Tuesday. Discussion during the meeting covered US-Thailand cooperation on space technology, climate change, global warming and natural crises, Settapong said. Thailand’s space agency will support the Royal Thai Armed Forces under a technology-sharing agreement announced on Monday. Under the agreement, the Geo-Informatics and Space Technology Development Agency (GISTDA) will share surveillance and other data to enhance national security and support missions by the military, GISTDA spokesman Siam Lawawirojwong said. The space agency will also boost skills among military personnel to protect Thailand&rsquo;s interests over land, sea, air and space, he added. #### TechNews (科技新報) (press, zh, newsroom)

- undated — 攜手 Lonestar 推進低地球軌道資料中心建置，群聯推出太空專用 SSD 解決方案
  攜手 Lonestar 推進低地球軌道資料中心建置，群聯推出太空專用 SSD 解決方案 | TechNews 科技新報 群聯電子 20 日宣布，正式推出全新「Pascari Space」太空專用 SSD 解決方案，全面進軍商業太空應用與太空軌道基礎設施儲存市場，該系列新產品亦同步於本週舉行的 SC26 超級電腦大會中公開亮相。 群聯指出，隨著地面資料中心面臨電力耗損、散熱不易、空間受限與天災等挑戰，加上太空發射成本顯著下滑，推動環境監測、科學研究與衛星運算等新興太空經濟成形，市場對高可靠度與高效能太空儲存方案的需求隨之大幅增長。群聯結合超過 26 年的研發經驗與經過航太任務驗證的技術，並透過旗下 Phison IMAGIN+ 客製化服務與 ASIC 設計量身打造，協助客戶克服太空極端環境帶來的嚴苛考驗。 獲太空任務驗證，達最高成熟度 TRL-9 級別 群聯在航太領域具備深厚實績，旗下儲存技術不僅已搭載於 NASA 火星探測車（Mars Rover），更於 2025 年攜手 Lonestar Data Holdings，導入全球首批部署於月球的資料中心硬體。這些成果驗證了 Pascari SSD 的太空實績，並使其成功達到技術成熟度第 9 級（TRL-9）標準。 群聯執行長潘健成指出，太空正迅速成為下一個核心資料基礎設施領域，其飛行任務面臨的挑戰遠高於地面系統。Pascari Space 系列讓客戶能依特定任務需求客製化儲存配置，不再受限於固定規格，為下一代太空基礎設施建立關鍵核心技術。 任務導向三大產品線，深度防護應對極端環境 針對太空任務不可逆且故障難以修復的特性，Pascari Space 規劃了三大產品線： 1.Flight Critical（飛行關鍵型儲存）：採用寬溫設計的 DRAM-less 架構，專為航太器中最核心位置設計，將系統可靠度置於極致效能之上。 2.Main Storage（主儲存）：適用於一般系統與機載運算設備，兼顧耐用度與運算效能表現。 3. Orbital Data Center（太空軌道資料中心儲存）：提供大容量配置，滿足軌道資料中心與海量
  https://technews.tw/2026/11/20/phison-launches-ssd-solution-designed-for-space/

  The SSG Landers announced Sunday they have retained their own free agent pitcher Moon Seung-won on a multiyear contract. The Landers said Moon agreed to a two-year pact with an option for a third season. He will receive 750 million won ($557,000) in total salary and a signing bonus of 500 million won. He can make another 250 million won in incentives. The 36-year-old has only pitched for the Landers franchise since making his Korea Baseball Organization debut in 2012, when they were called the SK Wyverns under different ownership. He helped the club win Korean Series titles in 2018 and 2022. In 2026, the right-hander matched his career high with 62 appearances, going 2-2 with 17 holds and two saves and a 3.45 ERA over 62 2/3 innings — his best ERA in a healthy season since 2020. - Sun, 22 Nov 2026 11:57:02 GMT — Sogang University, KIST join forces on research, talent development
  Sogang University and the Korea Institute of Science and Technology (KIST) have signed a memorandum of understanding to expand joint research and foster specialized talent in semiconductors, clean hydrogen and space-related composite materials. The agreement was signed by Sogang President Sim Jong-hyeok and KIST President Oh Sang-rok at the Sogang’s Seoul campus on Nov. 11, the universities said. The partnership will combine KIST’s mission-oriented research capabilities with Sogang’s academic and educational strengths to advance strategic technologies and nurture the next generation of researchers. “Our partnership with KIST, which has been at the forefront of science and technology development in Korea, will further strengthen Sogang’s research and educational capabilities,” Sim said. - undated — 광속 1일 거리…보이저 1호가 ‘픽’한 태양계 사진첩 [놀라운 우주] 역대 우주선 중 지구에서 가장 먼 우주를 날아가고 있는 미국항공우주국(나사)의 성간 우주 탐사선 보이저 1호가 지난 18일 오후 7시16분(한국시각) 1광일 거리의 우주 공간을 통과하는 역사적인 이정표를 세웠다.1광일 거리는 초속 30만km인 빛의 속도로 꼬박 하루 걸...
  광속 1일 거리…보이저 1호가 ‘픽’한 태양계 사진첩 [놀라운 우주] 본문 미래&amp;과학 과학 광속 1일 거리…보이저 1호가 ‘픽’한 태양계 사진첩 [놀라운 우주] 곽노필 기자 수정 2026-11-22 13:58 펼침 0:00 Your browser does not support the audio element. 곽노필의 미래창 화산 위성에서 ‘창백한 푸른 점’까지 광속 하루 거리 우주 비행의 기록들 인류 최초의 성간 우주탐사선 보이저 1호가 49년만인 2026년 11월18일 광속으로 하루 꼬박 걸리는 우주 공간을 통과한 것을 상징하는 그림. 나사 제공 광고 역대 우주선 중 지구에서 가장 먼 우주를 날아가고 있는 미국항공우주국(나사)의 성간 우주 탐사선 보이저 1호가 지난 18일 오후 7시16분(한국시각) 1광일 거리의 우주 공간을 통과하는 역사적인 이정표를 세웠다. 1광일 거리는 초속 30만km인 빛의 속도로 꼬박 하루 걸리는 거리를 뜻한다. 지구로부터 259억206만8356km 떨어진 거리가 이에 해당한다. 나사는 캘리포니아에 있는 제트추진연구소 보이저 1호 관제팀이, 우주선이 1광일 거리 지점을 통과하면서 보낸 신호를 24시간만에 확인했다고 밝혔다. 1977년 9월5일 발사된 보이저 1호가 여기까지 오는 데 걸린 시간은 49년 2개월11일이다. 이제 지구에서 보이저 1호에 명령을 보내면, 48시간 후에 응답을 받을 수 있다. 광고 1977년 9월18일 보이저 1호가 지구에서 1166만km 떨어진 곳에서 촬영한 지구와 달 사진. 두 천체가 함께 찍힌 최초의 사진이다. 나사 제공 지구와 달, 한 장의 사진에 처음 담기다 인류가 베일에 싸인 미지의 우주에 보낸 최전방 척후병이라 할 보이저 1호는, 그동안 우주 또는 세계에 대한 인식의 지평을 넓혀주는 귀중한 사진을 여럿 보내왔다.

What began as a mission to explore the outer planets has become one of humanity’s longest-running scientific adventures, with the spacecraft now travelling through interstellar space. And for astrophysicist and University of Auckland Professor of Physics Jan Eldridge, that adventure helped inspire her own career. NASA's Voyager 1 spacecraft is depicted in this artist's concept traveling through interstellar space, or the space between stars, which it entered in 2012.
